9. Any person opting for registration under rule 14A shall undergo OTP based authentication of Aadhaar number. 
10. For an application under rule 14B: 
Rule 14B of CGST Rules, 2017 provides a simplified GST registration mechanism for small sellers who 
supplies goods exclusively through electronical commerce operator’s platform subject to fulfilment of certain 
conditions, inter-alia, as mentioned below: 
(1) Single registration under the regular taxpayer category (registered through FORM GST REG-01 
except composition, ISD, SEZ Developer, SEZ Unit and casual Taxpayer) shall be permitted in a State or 
Union territory against a single PAN under rule 14B. 
(1) Application shall be submitted where he has no physical presence and no active regular registration in 
the State or Union territory. 
(iii) An application shall, inter alia, be permitted where no proceedings under section 29 have been initiated 
or no application for cancellation of registration has been filed in respect of the registration in the home 
State or Union territory. 
(iv) An application shall be applicable in respect of jurisdictions other than the Other Territory category. 
(v) The GSTIN of the electronic commerce operator entered in the application shall be active and under 
the regular taxpayer category. The associated TCS registration on the same PAN shall also be active in the 
State or Union territory where the registration is sought. 
(vi) The selected electronic commerce operator may provide consent for the Principal Place of Business 
(PPoB) and, where applicable, the Additional Place of Business (APoB) within seven working days. 
Where consent for the PPoB is not received within the prescribed period, the ARN shall not be generated, 
and applicant may submit application again. In the case of an APoB, where consent is not received within 
the prescribed period, such APoB shall be deleted and the ARN shall be generated, subject to receipt of 
consent for the PPoB and successful completion of Biometric Aadhaar authentication and document 
verification, as applicable. 
(vii) After submission of the application, the Primary Authorised Signatory (PAS) of the selected home 
State registration shall select the Promoter/Partner for Aadhaar authentication. The PAS and the selected 
Promoter/Partner shall thereafter undergo Biometric Aadhaar authentication and document verification, 
where the same have not been successfully completed earlier. 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 143 


29. 


30. 


31. 


(viii) It is advised that the electronic commerce operator should not file any application for 
amendment after providing consent for the PPoB and/or APoB and until approval of the registration. After 
grant of registration to the supplier, an application for amendment may be filed by e-commerce operator. 
However, before filing such application for amendment, the e-commerce operator shall withdraw the 
consent linked to all the concerned addresses. 
(ix) Whereas the electronic commerce operator’s application for change of the address declared as the 
registered person’s principal place of business has been approved, and the registered person, being required 
to amend its registration accordingly, has failed to file the requisite amendment application within thirty 
days of such approval, the registration granted under this rule shall be deemed to be cancelled 
electronically by common portal and intimated to the said person in FORM GST REG-38. In case the 
said address had been declared as an Additional Place of Business by the said registered person and the 
said registered person failed to submit the requisite amendment application within the prescribed period 
of thirty days, the details of such Additional Place of Business shall be deleted from the certificate of 
registration. 
(x) After grant of registration, where such registration is cancelled or suspended, no fresh registration 
under rule 14B shall be permitted. 
(xi) Where applications under rule 14B are under process in multiple States or Union territories, and a 
field visit associated with the GSTIN of the selected home State, in connection with any such application, 
results in negative remarks, all such applications pending under rule 14B shall be auto-rejected in FORM 
GST REG-05A. 
11. Status of the application filed online can be tracked on the common portal by entering Application Reference 
Number (ARN) indicated on the Acknowledgment. 
12. No fee is payable for filing application for registration. 
13. Authorised signatory shall not be a minor. 
14. Any person having multiple places of business within a State, requiring a separate registration for any of its 
places of business shall need to apply separately in respect of each place of business 
15. After approval of application, registration certificate shall be made available on the common portal. 
16. Temporary Reference Number (TRN) will be allotted after successfully furnishing preliminary details in PART 
—A of the application which can be used for filling up details in PART-B of the application. TRN will be available 
on the common portal for a period of 15 days. 
17. Any person who applies for registration under rule 8 may give an option to pay tax under section 10 in Part B 
of FORM GST REG-01, which shall be considered as an intimation to pay tax under the said section. 
18. Government departments applying for registration as suppliers may not furnish Bank Account details. 
19. Taxpayers who want to pay tax by availing benefit of notification No. 2/2019 — Central Tax (Rate) dated 
07.03.2019, as amended, shall indicate such option at serial no. 5 and 6.1 (iii) of this Form.”. 
In the principal rules, in FORM GST REG-03, - 
(1) after the word, letters and figure “FORM GST REG-03”, for the brackets, words, figures and letter “[See 
rules 9(2), 19(2) and 14A]”, the brackets, words, figures and letters “[See rules 9(2), 14A, 14B, 19 and 20]7 
shall be substituted; 
(1) for the words “You are directed”, the words “You are requested” shall be substituted; 
(iii) the words and brackets “You are hereby directed to appear before the undersigned on ......... 
(DD/MM/YYYY) at ....... HH:MM)” shall be omitted; 
(iv) before the paragraph starting with the words “If no response”, a paragraph with the following words and 
brackets shall be inserted, namely: - 
“Tf you wish to be heard in person, or through an authorised representative, an opportunity of Personal 
Hearing is hereby granted to appear before the undersigned on ......... (DD/MM/YYYY) at ....... 
HH:MM).”.

shall be substituted;

/ 14B” shall be substituted.

INSTRUCTIONS 


List of all statements/declarations/undertakings/certificates and other supporting documents to be provided along with the refund application may be found in the Annexure 
appended to these instructions. 


In cases where the credit has accumulated both on account of rate of tax on inputs being higher than the rate of tax on output supplies, and output supplies being made under 
a concessional notification due to which the rate of tax on output supplies is less than the rate of tax on inputs, separate refund applications shall be filed, distinctly identifying 
the accumulation under each category. In such cases, it shall be ensured that the details of outward supplies pertaining to the relevant category are furnished exclusively in 
the respective statement, namely Statement 1A or Statement 1B. Further, outward supply details do not have to correspond exactly to the inward-supply details in the same 
row. 

Item-wise details of supplies are required to be filled in Annexure-3 in case of refund of unutilised input tax credit. 

Debit entry shall be made in electronic credit or cash ledger at the time of filing the application. 

Acknowledgement in FORM GST RFD-02 will be issued if the application is found complete in all respects. 

Claim of refund on export of goods with payment of IGST shall not be processed through this application. 

Bank account details should be as per registration data. Any change in bank details shall first be amended in registration particulars before quoting in the application. 

In case of refund claim of accumulated input tax credit on account of inverted duty structure, net input tax credit means input tax credit availed on inputs and input services 
(in respect of which input tax credit has been availed on or after 1st November 2026). 

In case of refund claim of accumulated input tax credit on account of zero-rated supplies, net input tax credit means input tax credit availed on inputs and input services. 
Adjusted total turnover shall have the same meaning as defined in rule 89(4)(E). 

For the purpose of Statement-1A, refund claim will be based on supplies reported in GSTR-1 as amended by GSTR-1A, if any. 

BRC or FIRC details will be mandatory where refund is claimed against export of services, while details of shipping bill and EGM will be mandatory to be provided in case 
of exporting goods. 

Where the invoice details are amended (including export), refund shall be allowed as per the calculation based on amended value. 

Details of export made without payment of tax shall be reported in Statement-3.
